The default texts are `messages['empty.emptyTitle' …]`. `ErrorState` is the error variant with `caseId` (shown after `messages['empty.caseId']` in monospace, selectable so it can be copied) and a `reportAction` slot; the retry is its `action`. A test proves the error icon is never the empty icon.
- **2026-09-28 — ProgressBar, Skeleton and Stepper (P2.5c).** `ProgressBar` takes a required `label` (its accessible name), `value` and `max` (default 100): Mantine Progress size 'sm', 5px high, fully rounded (owner), the P2.1 primitive's colours and its fill from the leading edge (it flips in right-to-left). `Skeleton` is the P2.1 primitive (radius md, pulsing, hidden from assistive technology) with layout classes. `Stepper` takes `steps` (label, optional description), `active` (earlier steps are completed; `steps.length` completes all) and `onStepClick`, which makes every step a button (Mantine `allowNextStepsSelect` is true by default). It is an ordered list; the current step is `aria-current="step"`, and a completed step says so to assistive technology (`messages['stepper.completed']`).
- **2026-09-28 — Values taken from Mantine defaults (P2.5c).** `@mantine/core` 9.6.2 Stepper.css and defaults, size 'sm' with the owner's 32px icons: horizontal, the icon at the start and the label beside it, wrapping with 16px between rows; the step icon round with a 2px border and the number in bold at 13px (the size's font); completed filled with `brand.solid` and a white check at 60%, the current one ringed in `border.brand`, the others on `surface.disabled` with a `border.subtle` ring (Mantine gray-2 / dark-5 for both) and the number in `text.secondary` (gray-7 / dark-1); 2px lines between steps with 16px on each side, `border.subtle`, and `border.brand` up to the current step; colour changes in 150ms (our base duration); the label 13px, weight 600, line height 1, 12px after the icon; the description 12px (Mantine: the label size minus 2px, 11px; A.5's smallest is 12px), line height 1, `text.secondary`, 4px (12 / 3) above and below. Progress.css: 'sm' is 5px; the track `surface.sunken` (Mantine gray-2 / dark-4).
- **2026-10-01 — Cards and KeyValueList redesigned (P3.2a, decided by the owner; a deliberate change from the old system).** The old look (upper-case labels above values, an icon and a line in the SectionCard header) read as a form rather than a record; the new one reads like a statement, label and value on one line, and scans down a column. **SectionCard:** the title and an optional description at the start, the actions at the end; no icon by default (`icon` stays optional) and no line under the header (`withDivider` is removed); the header has padding md at the top and sides, the body follows md below it (with `flush`, the header keeps md at the bottom too). **KeyValueList, layout "rows" (default):** each item one row, the label at the start (13px, `text.secondary`, as written — never upper case or letter-spaced, in any script, so the `:lang()` rule of P2.8a is gone), the value at the end (13px, weight 500, `text.primary`, tabular digits for `numeric`), long values wrapping and staying at the end, the label at most half the row; 8px above and below each row; a 1px `border.subtle` line between rows and none after the last row of a column (`keyValueLines`, tested: items fill the columns row by row, a `fullWidth` item is a row of its own under every column). `columns` 1, 2 or 3 (default 2; 4 is removed) from 48em, one column on phones. `fullWidth` items span the columns with the label above the value. `groups` (title and items) splits the list into titled groups, each a `role="group"` named by its title (12px, semibold, `text.secondary`). **Layout "stacked"**: the label above the value (2px apart), no lines, for short cards. Empty "—" and loading skeletons as before. A text value is isolated (`bdi`), so Latin in a right-to-left page keeps its order ("d.o.o.", "12.345,60 EUR") while the row keeps the page's alignment — the review of the right-to-left baselines found ".Alfa Trade d.o.o" and "EUR 12.345,60" without it. **Values chosen without an owner's value or Mantine default** (reported to the owner): 16px between columns (md, Mantine SimpleGrid's default spacing, as before), 24px (lg) above each group after the first, 8px under a group title in the stacked layout (in "rows" the row's own 8px does it).
- **2026-10-01 — KeyValueList: 32px between columns in "rows" (decided by the owner, P3.3).** With 16px the value at the end of one column visually ran into the label at the start of the next, so the "rows" layout now has 32px (spacing xl) between columns; the stacked layout keeps 16px. The owner kept 24px above a group and 8px under a group title.

## Navigation

- **2026-09-28 — P2.6 split.** P2.6 is split into P2.6a (Tabs, Breadcrumbs, CursorPagination, ShortcutHint) and P2.6b (CommandPalette), each with its own pull request (BUILD-PLAN rule 6).
- **2026-09-28 — The previous Design System's navigation (decided by the owner).** Carried over exactly; everything not listed is the Mantine 9 default with Liro tokens:
  1. **Tabs:** radius md; panels are not kept mounted while hidden (`keepMounted` false), so dialogs belong at page level, outside Tabs (AGENTS.md D16); the tab list is always centred, because a list at the start of a wide screen leaves a gap that looks like something is missing. (FormTabs, since 2026-10-02, P3.6: start-aligned as the first row of the form card.)
  2. **Breadcrumbs:** the separator "›" (U+203A, which mirrors by itself in right-to-left), separator margin xs; links underlined only on hover; the last item is not a link.
  3. **CursorPagination:** the old system used Mantine Pagination at size sm, radius md, without first and last buttons; that look stays for the previous and next controls; there are no page numbers.
  4. **CommandPalette** (the old one was Mantine Spotlight): opens with Ctrl/Cmd+K and also Ctrl/Cmd+P; radius lg; a search field with an 18px search icon at the start and a placeholder from the provider's messages; the matching text in results highlighted; results in groups, actions first, then navigation ("Go to"); each item with a label, an optional description, an optional 17px icon and optional keywords used for matching; "Nothing found" from the provider's messages. **ShortcutHint** (Mantine Kbd): size xs.
- **2026-09-28 — Tabs, Breadcrumbs, CursorPagination, ShortcutHint (P2.6a).** `Tabs` takes `items` (value, label, content, optional icon and `disabled`), controlled or uncontrolled value (default: the first) and a `label` for the tab list; it is the P2.1 primitive (Mantine Tabs, variant default, radius md) with the list centred; Radix mounts only the active panel. `Breadcrumbs` takes `items` (label, `href`); it is a `nav` named by `messages['breadcrumbs.label']` with an ordered list; links are the provider's `linkComponent` (so the application's router handles them), in `text.link`, underlined on hover; the last item is `aria-current="page"` and never a link; the separator "›" is decoration (`aria-hidden`), 10px on each side, in `text.secondary` (Mantine gray-7 / dark-2), and the right-to-left baselines show it pointing left. `CursorPagination` takes `hasPrevious`, `hasNext`, `onPrevious`, `onNext` and a `count` slot; the controls are named by `messages['table.previous' | 'table.next']` and their chevrons mirror in right-to-left. `ShortcutHint` takes the `keys` in the user's words (the application knows the platform) and joins them with "+".
- **2026-09-28 — Values taken from Mantine defaults (P2.6a).** `@mantine/core` 9.6.2: Breadcrumbs.css (items line height 1 and not wrapping, the list wrapping; separator margin xs = 10px); Anchor (the link colour; hover underline, the owner's); Pagination.css size 'sm' (controls 26px square, 1px border gray-4 / dark-4 → `border.strong`, background white / dark-6 → `surface.raised`, hover gray-0 / dark-5 → `surface.hover`, radius md, icons 26 / 1.8 = 14.4px, 8px apart; disabled Mantine opacity 0.4 → `surface.disabled` and `text.disabled`, B.6); Kbd.css size 'xs' (monospace, bold, radius sm, 1px border with a 3px bottom edge in gray-3 / dark-4 → `border.default`, text gray-7 / dark-0 → `text.secondary`, background gray-0 / dark-6 → `surface.hover`, padding 0.12em 0.45em, line height 1.55, `unicode-bidi: embed`). **Departure:** Kbd 'xs' is 10px; A.5's smallest size is 12px (AGENTS.md D15), so the key text is 12px, as field descriptions (Mantine 11px) became 12px.
- **2026-09-28 — CommandPalette (P2.6b).** `CommandPalette` takes `items` (id, label, optional description, 17px icon and keywords, `group` 'actions' or 'navigation', `onSelect`), controlled `open`/`onOpenChange`, and for server search `onSearch` (after `searchDelay`, default 300ms as ComboboxField) with `loading`; without `onSearch` it filters the items itself: every word of the query must appear in the label, the description or a keyword, ignoring case and accents (`commandMatches`, tested). Ctrl/Cmd+K and Ctrl/Cmd+P open it anywhere on the page (`opensPalette`, tested; Ctrl+P replaces the browser's print dialog, as the owner's old palette did). Actions are listed first, then "Go to"; the typed words are highlighted in the labels (`highlightParts`, tested; case-insensitive, accents as written). On cmdk (`shouldFilter` off): the focus stays in the search field, the arrows move, Enter runs the item and closes, Escape closes and returns the focus. Texts: `messages['command.title' | 'command.placeholder' | 'command.actions' | 'command.navigation' | 'command.noResults']`, and `field.loading`. "Nothing found" and "loading" stand outside the list (a listbox may hold only groups and options); the list is always rendered, hidden when empty, because the search field points to it with `aria-controls`.
- **2026-09-28 — Values taken from Mantine defaults (P2.6b).** `@mantine/spotlight` 9.6.2 (styles.css and defaults): 80px from the top (`yOffset`), the Modal's 440px, radius lg (the owner's); the search an Input size 'lg' without border or background: 50px high, 16px text, the 18px icon (the owner's) in a 48px section at the start; the results under a 1px line (gray-2 / dark-4 → `border.default`), 4px padding, at most 400px high (`maxHeight`) and scrolling; an item 7px by 16px, radius md, `surface.hover` under the pointer (gray-0 / dark-6), `brand.solid` with `brand.onSolid` when chosen by the keyboard; the icon 16px before the label; the description 12px in `text.secondary`, and on the brand fill in `brand.onSolid` (Mantine lowers it with opacity 0.7, which Appendix B.6 forbids for text); a group label 12px, bold, upper case, `text.secondary`, 16px from the start and 16px above the group; "nothing found" `text.secondary`, centred, 16px padding. The highlight is Mantine's Highlight (a Mark, yellow by default); no meaning is yellow, so it is `status.warning.bg` (orange-0, a pale yellow; in dark the warning tint) under `text.primary`.

## Table

- **2026-09-30 — The previous Design System's DataTable (decided by the owner).** Carried over exactly; everything not listed is the Mantine 9 default with Liro tokens:
  1. **Look:** table font size sm (13px); cells 12px (sm) top and bottom, 16px (md) at the sides; the header row on `surface.sunken`, sticky on request (always when virtualized, P3.2); rows 44px high when virtualized (P3.2); minimum column width 64px; the table exactly as wide as its columns, so resizing one column never shifts the others (P3.2), scrolling sideways inside its container below 640px; a row highlights under the pointer only when rows can be pressed; selected rows on `surface.selected`; the selection column with an xs checkbox, the header checkbox indeterminate when some rows are selected.
  2. **Sort:** a sortable header is a plain button (no button look) in the header's size and weight: the label, then a 13px icon 4px after it — ArrowUpDown unsorted, ArrowUp ascending, ArrowDown descending; an end-aligned column aligns the whole group to the end, the icon still after the label. Unsorted: the header's text colour; sorted: label and icon in `text.brand` (neutral since 2026-10-02, P3.6: text.primary, the unsorted icon text.tertiary). A press sorts another column ascending, the ascending column descending, the descending one ascending again (never back to unsorted). `aria-sort` (none / ascending / descending) on sortable header cells only; other columns have no icon and no `aria-sort`.
  3. **Loading, two states, never a translucent overlay:** the first load (no rows yet) shows `skeletonRows` (default 5) skeleton bars, each 36px high, radius sm, 8px (xs) apart, inside 16px (md) padding, under the header; a refetch (rows already shown) keeps the rows as they are, with a 14px loader in the top end corner of the table, and nothing dimmed. (Moved on 2026-10-02, P3.6: above the table at the end, in a reserved slot, with "Updating…".)
  4. **Totals row** (values from the application): a `tfoot` row on `surface.sunken`, semibold (600), a 1px `border.strong` line above it, sticky at the bottom edge while the table scrolls; amounts tabular and aligned like their column; an optional label in the first column when that column has no total of its own; empty cells stay empty. The editable grid (P3.4) uses a 2px line.
  5. **Filters in P3.1: state and clearing only.** `filters` decides whether an empty table shows "nothing here yet" or "no rows match"; the second offers "Clear filters", which clears them. No filter icon in headers and no `filterable` flag on columns: filters are defined in FilterBar (P3.3). **This replaces the plan's `filterable` column flag.**
  6. **Phone cards** (P3.2): by real branching, never both layouts rendered, with the card values the owner gave for P3.2.
- **2026-09-30 — DataTable (P3.1).** `DataTable` takes `columns` (id, header, `cell(row)`, `align`, `numeric` for tabular digits, `sortable`), `rows` in the order to show, `getRowId`, `getRowLabel` (names each row's checkbox and menu for assistive technology: "Select F-2026-114", "Actions: F-2026-114") and a `label` (the table's accessible name). **Controlled, and never sorts, filters or sums** (unit tests prove the rows keep their order under any `sort`, and that only given totals appear): `sort`/`onSortChange` (`nextSort`, `ariaSort`, tested); `filters`/`onFiltersChange` (`hasActiveFilters`: a filter counts unless null, undefined, '', false or an empty list); `selection`/`onSelectionChange` with `bulkActions` in the P2.7 BulkActionBar above the table (its "Select all N" is `onSelectAll`, with an exact `count`); `onRowClick`; `rowActions` (a DropdownMenu from a 28px CompactIconButton with the `more` intent, in a last column); `totals` per column id with `totalsLabel`; cursor paging through CursorPagination, with the count from `formatCount` (the exact number up to `countThreshold`, default 10,000 = `COUNT_THRESHOLD`, then "More than 10,000", through `messages['table.count']`; `countIsExact` false reads as a lower bound); `loading` with `skeletonRows`; `emptyAction` (the first step of "nothing here yet"); `rowLimitMessage` (a slot under the table); `exportAction` (a slot above it at the end: the application runs the export as a job); `stickyHeader` and `maxHeight`. TanStack Table builds the rows, cells and selection (`rowSelectionFeature` only); no row model sorts or filters. New messages: `table.clearFilters`, `table.selectAll`, `table.selectRow`, `table.rowActions`; the refetch loader is a `role="status"` with `field.loading`.
- **2026-09-30 — Pressing a row, and the keyboard.** A row that can be pressed takes the focus (`tabIndex` 0, focus ring inside the row) and opens with Enter as well as the pointer. A press on a control inside the row (checkbox, menu button, a link in a cell), or in the row menu, which renders in a portal but bubbles through React, does not press the row. Rows that cannot be pressed are neither focusable nor highlighted.
- **2026-09-30 — Scrolling inside the table.** The table sits in its own scroll area (sideways below 640px; both ways inside `maxHeight`). While its content overflows, the area is focusable and named by the table's label, so it can be scrolled with the keyboard (WCAG 2.1.1, axe `scrollable-region-focusable`); a table that fits adds no tab stop (measured with a ResizeObserver). A sticky header or totals row sticks inside that area, so `stickyHeader` needs `maxHeight` (P3.2's virtual list has its own height). With a sticky header or totals, the area has 48px scroll padding at that edge, so a row reached by the keyboard is never hidden under them (WCAG 2.4.11). Cells use `border-collapse: separate` with the lines on the cells, so the sticky header and totals keep their lines (Mantine redraws them with shadows for the same reason).
- **2026-09-30 — Values taken from Mantine defaults (P3.1).** `@mantine/core` 9.6.2 Table.css and Table defaults: `withRowBorders` true (a 1px line under each row, gray-3 / dark-4 → `border.default`), none under the last body row; no table border, no column borders, no stripes; header cells aligned to the start; header weight bold (700): Mantine sets none, so the browser's `th` weight applies; the hover colour gray-1 / dark-5 → `surface.sunken`, as for the highlighted menu item (surface.hover, gray0, is 1.04:1 on white); the sticky header above the rows (Mantine z-index 3; here 10, the raised layer). Checkbox.css size 'xs': 16px, radius sm, the mark 60% (10px), with an invisible 4px margin so the target is 24px (WCAG 2.5.8). The refetch loader is Loader 'oval' at 14px: a 1.75px ring (14 / 8) in the primary colour (`border.brand`) with one quarter open, as the toast's loader. The empty states are EmptyState at its normal size (Mantine 'md').

- **2026-09-30 — P3.2 decisions (the owner).** (1) The table becomes cards below **48em (768px)**, the breakpoint of the old DataTable and FilterBar (and of KeyValueList's one column). (2) **The column-resize handle, carried over exactly:** an invisible 9px strip on the header cell's end edge (`inset-inline-end: -4px`), the header's full height, cursor `col-resize`, no text selection, `touch-action: none`; inside it a 3px, fully rounded line, 64% of the header's height, vertically centred (top 18%), 3px from the strip's start, transparent by default; while the pointer is anywhere over the header the line shows in `border.default`, so the column reads as resizable before the strip is found; while the pointer is on the strip or it has keyboard focus (and during a drag) the line is `brand.solid`, and that rule is more specific than the header rule so grey never wins; colour transition base / standard. Keyboard: `role="separator"`, `aria-orientation="vertical"`, `aria-label` "Resize column: <label>", `aria-valuenow` / `min` / `max`, focusable; the arrows change the width by 10px (Shift 40px) and the arrow that widens points in the reading direction (ArrowLeft in right-to-left). Widths between 64px (or the column's own minimum) and 640px. With resizing on, text that does not fit ends with an ellipsis instead of running into the next column. (3) **A deliberate addition to the old behaviour (WCAG 2.2, 2.5.7, dragging):** a press on the handle without moving opens a small popover with two CompactIconButtons, "Narrower" and "Wider" (−40px / +40px, the Shift+arrow step), each named with the column ("Narrower: Customer"); a button is disabled at the limit; Escape or a press outside closes it and the focus returns to the handle. Dragging and the arrows work as before.
- **2026-09-30 — DataTable on phones, long lists and resizing (P3.2).**
  - **Cards, by real branching** (Appendix B.5): below 48em only the cards are rendered, never the table hidden with CSS (`matchMedia`, read with `useSyncExternalStore`; on the server the table). `layout` 'table' or 'cards' forces one (the stories use it: the test viewport is 1280px wide). A card is described by `mobile`: `title` (default `getRowLabel`), `subtitle`, `badge`, and `details`, the column ids shown as label and value (default every column, their header and cell). The card is the owner's (border, radius md, 12px padding, raised; selected: `surface.selected` with `border.brand`; the checkbox, title and subtitle each on one line, the badge and the 28px row menu at the end; detail labels 12px `text.tertiary`, values 12px weight 500, tabular, 2px apart). A card that can be pressed takes the focus and opens with Enter, as a row. The cards are a list (`ul`) named by the table's label. The totals, when given, stand under the cards on `surface.sunken`, semibold, with the 1px `border.strong` line above, one line per total (the column's header and value) under the `totalsLabel`. **Sorting has no control on a phone yet** (there are no headers): it belongs with the FilterBar on phones (P3.3).
  - **Virtualization** (`virtualize`, TanStack Virtual 3.14.13): only the rows in view are drawn, inside `maxHeight`, with 8 rows of overscan and spacer rows above and below that keep the scroll height; table rows are 44px (owner) with their text on one line, the header always sticky; cards are measured once drawn (estimated 104px, owner), the 16px gap part of each item. Before the scroll area is measured (and on the server) a 720px high view is assumed, so the first rows show at once. Assistive technology hears the full size: `aria-rowcount` on the table and `aria-rowindex` on each row (header 1, totals last); `aria-setsize` / `aria-posinset` on each card.
  - **Resizing** (`resizable`; per column `width`, `minWidth`, `resizable: false`, and `label` for the names when the header is not text; `onColumnWidthsChange` reports the widths after each resize for the application to keep). The starting width is the column's `width`, else the width its content takes on one line, measured in an invisible first layout before the browser paints (`width: max-content`, no wrapping), rounded up and kept between the limits; it is measured again when the web fonts finish loading (`document.fonts` `loadingdone`), until the user resizes, because the first measurement can run with a narrower fallback font. Then the table has `table-layout: fixed` with a `colgroup`, and its width is the sum of its columns (plus 48px for the checkbox column and 44px for the menu column), so resizing one column moves none of the others. Appendix B.5 found `table-layout: fixed` worse for columns sized by their content; here every column has an explicit width, which is what the fixed layout is for. The drag follows the pointer from where it was pressed, measured from the leading edge (`widthAfterDrag`), with pointer capture; a press that moves less than 3px is a click and opens the popover. The logic (`clampWidth`, `widthAfterKey`, `widthAfterDrag`) is unit-tested in both directions; the "Resizable columns" story resizes with the keyboard and the popover in all four modes. New messages: `table.resizeColumn`, `table.narrower`, `table.wider`.
  - **Two lint exceptions, in `eslint.config.mjs`, one file each (AGENTS.md W8):** `data-table-resize.tsx` may put `tabIndex` and handlers on its `separator` (the WAI-ARIA window-splitter pattern: a focusable separator is an interactive widget in ARIA 1.2, which jsx-a11y does not know); `data-table.tsx` turns off `react-hooks/incompatible-library`, which only reports that the React Compiler would skip a component using TanStack Virtual's `useVirtualizer` (the package is not built with the compiler).
- **2026-09-30 — 1,000 rows stay responsive (P3.2, BUILD-PLAN "Done when").** Measured on the "1,000 rows (virtualized)" and "1,000 cards" stories in the built Storybook, Chromium 153 (Playwright 1.63) on the owner's Windows machine, from the event to the next painted frame, five times each; "4× slower" is Chrome's CPU throttling, standing in for a mid-range phone. Table: sorting (including the story's own sort of 1,000 rows, the application's work) 26–61ms, 4× slower 138–211ms; selecting a row 13–16ms, 4× slower 100–146ms; a 2,000px scroll jump 29–36ms, 4× slower 238–316ms. Cards: selecting 9–17ms, 4× slower 61–112ms; a 2,000px scroll jump 36–55ms, 4× slower 310–513ms (every card on screen is new and measured). Appendix B.5 measured 120ms per interaction with branching and virtualization, and 1,592ms without, at 932 rows.
- **2026-09-30 — Values taken from Mantine defaults (P3.2).** Where the owner gave no value: the gaps inside a card (between the checkbox, the title and the end items, and between the top row and the details) and between the cards are Mantine Group and Stack's default gap, md (16px); the resize popover is the P2.1 popover (padding 12px 16px) with its two buttons 16px apart (Group); the buttons' icons are lucide ChevronsRightLeft ("Narrower") and ChevronsLeftRight ("Wider") at the CompactIconButton's 16px.

## Filters

- **2026-10-01 — FilterBar (P3.3, decided by the owner; old Design System values where noted, everything else Mantine 9 defaults with Liro tokens).**
  1. **Filter kinds:** select, multiSelect, dateRange, numberRange (min–max as decimal strings), boolean (a yes / no select) and text.
  2. **Layout (the old Toolbar):** search and filters on the start side, actions on the end side, space between them; the row wraps; 12px (sm) between items, 8px (xs) between actions; inside a card (`inCard`) 16px (md) at the top and sides and 8px (xs) at the bottom. **The row aligns to the bottom** (flex-end), so the search field, the inline filters and the buttons stand on one control line although the filters have labels above.
  3. **Search (the old Toolbar):** 260px on desktop, 100% below the xs breakpoint (36em); a 15px search icon at the start; a clear button (the small close button, 28px, `messages['filter.clearSearch']`) at the end, only while there is text; placeholder `messages['filter.search']` ("Search…") unless given, and the same text as its accessible name. Reported after typing pauses (300ms, as ComboboxField), at once when cleared.
  4. **Inline filters:** `inline` (default 0) filters stand in the row on desktop, the rest are in a drawer opened by "Filters" (intent `filter`). **Each inline filter has a visible label above it** (as a normal field, 13px) and the same text as its placeholder while empty — what the old system did. A placeholder-only name disappears once a value is chosen, so the user could no longer tell which filter shows "Sent" or "01.09.–30.09."; WCAG also discourages placeholder-only labels. Widths: select and text 180px, multiSelect 220px, dateRange 240px, boolean 140px, number min and max 100px each; 100% on phones.
  5. **Drawer:** from the end side, 320px on desktop, 100% on phones, titled "Filters" (13px, bold), the controls stacked 12px (sm) apart. On phones (below 48em) every filter is in the drawer.
  6. **Pills:** active filters that are not inline show under the row as removable pills "Label: value" (a range "from – to"), plus "Clear all", a subtle xs button that clears only this bar's own filters (the application's other keys in `values` and the search stay).
  7. **Sort on phones** (cards have no headers): a neutral button like "Filters", with ArrowUpDown, whose text is the current sort kept short ("Date ↓"); it opens a menu with the sortable columns, a divider, then Ascending / Descending, each with a check mark. Long labels follow the button wrapping rule (P2.7d). Desktop keeps sorting in the column headers.
  8. **The bar only reports changes;** the application filters and sorts on the server.
- **2026-10-01 — FilterBar (P3.3), how it is built.** `FilterBar` takes `filters` (`FilterDefinition`: id, label, type and, for selects, options; `decimals` for a number range), `values` / `onValuesChange` (the same `DataTableFilters` record the DataTable reads, so "no rows match" and "Clear filters" work with it), `inline`, `search` / `onSearchChange` / `searchDelay` / `searchPlaceholder`, `sort` / `sortColumns` / `onSortChange`, `actions`, `inCard` and `layout` ('desktop' or 'phone' forces one, as DataTable's `layout`; default by the viewport, with the shared `usePhone`, moved out of DataTable). Values by kind: a string, a list of strings, a `DateRange`, a `NumberRange` ({min, max} decimal strings), true / false, or text; null, '', [] and an open range mean "not set" (`isFilterSet`, tested). The logic is in `filter-logic.ts`, tested: `isFilterSet`, `emptyFilterValue`, `clearFilters`, `filterValueText` (option labels, lists joined with ", ", yes / no from messages, dates through `format.date`, numbers through `format.number` with the filter's decimals, never rounded), `rangeText` ("from – to", "from –", "– to"), `sortButtonText`. A text filter is reported after the same pause as the search. A number range is a group named by its label, with two NumberFields named `messages['filter.rangeFrom' | 'filter.rangeTo'](label)`. The pills are a list; each remove button is named `messages['filter.remove'](label)`; the pill is Mantine Pill size 'sm' (22px, 12px text, fully rounded, as MultiSelectField's) in the variant "contrast" — the raised surface, `text.primary`, the remove × in `text.secondary` — **with a 1px `border.default` border (decided by the owner):** the remove button keeps the 24 × 24px minimum target and is named "Remove filter: <label>"; Mantine's default pill colour (gray-1) is exactly the page's, so the pills could not be seen, and the border keeps them visible on a white card too. The phone's sort menu is two radio groups of the menu primitive (column, direction), whose item now takes `check` to draw a check mark instead of the dot. New messages: `field.clear`, `filter.search`, `filter.clearSearch`, `filter.filters`, `filter.clearAll`, `filter.pill`, `filter.remove`, `filter.yes`, `filter.no`, `filter.rangeFrom`, `filter.rangeTo`, `filter.sort`, `filter.ascending`, `filter.descending`.
- **2026-10-01 — SelectField `clearable` (P3.3; Mantine Select's prop).** A filter must be emptied in place, and a Radix select cannot choose "nothing", so `clearable` puts the 28px close button (`messages['field.clear']`, the cancel intent's X) where the chevron is while a value is chosen; clearing reports `''`. SelectField now keeps its own value when uncontrolled, so the button also clears an uncontrolled field. Mantine draws its clear button in the input's right section the same way.

## Editable grid

- **2026-10-01 — EditableGrid (P3.4, decided by the owner; old Design System values where noted).**
  1. **Cells are always fields, as in the old grid:** unstyled inputs in a table with column lines (1px `border.default`), the header row on `surface.sunken`, compact cell padding (4px), numbers at the end with tabular digits (also in right-to-left). **The focused cell** has an inset 1px `border.focus` line and a **neutral** background, `surface.hover` — not the old blue `surface.selected`: blue is kept for actions and focus. A 44px column at the end holds the row's remove (×) button.
  2. **Enter (the old behaviour, as Excel):** Enter goes to the same column in the next row, and on the last row adds a row and moves into it; Shift+Enter goes to the same column in the previous row; Tab and Shift+Tab go to the next and previous cell in the browser's order. While a cell's option list is open (an account), Enter chooses the option and does not move; the next Enter moves. **On phones** (no Tab key) the on-screen keyboard's "next" (`enterkeyhint`) goes through every field of the row in order, then to the first field of the next row, and on the last field of the last row adds a row.
  3. **Row shortcuts** (the Business Central convention): Ctrl+Enter (Cmd+Enter on a Mac) inserts a row below the current one; Ctrl+Delete (Cmd+Delete on a Mac) deletes the current row. The focus stays in the same column: after a delete it moves to the next row, or to the previous one when the last row was deleted. No confirmation (the lines are a draft). The "Add line" button and the × buttons always remain, with the shortcuts shown beside "Add line" (ShortcutHint). `minRows` and `maxRows` are kept.
  4. **Errors and warnings per row:** a message line under the row, across its full width, 12px, with an icon in the tone's colour (danger, warning), linked to the row's fields with `aria-describedby`; the invalid cell gets the danger line (`status.danger.fg`). Visible without a mouse and without a tooltip. A row can show several messages, errors before warnings.
  5. **Totals** from props, shown with SettlingValue, under a 2px `border.strong` line (P3.1 used 1px for the DataTable). Nothing is computed in the grid.
- **2026-10-01 — EditableGrid (P3.4), how it is built.** `EditableGrid` takes `columns` (`EditableGridColumn`: id, header, width, `readOnly(row)`, and a kind: text, number with `decimals`, select with options, combobox with options or per-row options, `onSearch(rowId, query)` and `loading(row)`, date, or display — a value the application shows, such as the line amount), `rows` with `getRowId`, `onCellChange(rowId, columnId, value)`, `onAddRow(index)`, `onRemoveRow(rowId)`, `minRows` (default 1), `maxRows`, `messages` (`GridMessage`: tone, text, columns) by row id, `totals` (`GridTotal`: value, pending, currency, decimals) by column id, `totalsLabel`, a `footer` slot (a journal entry's balance) and `layout` ('desktop' or 'phone' forces the keyboard model; default by the viewport). The editors **are** the Design System's fields (TextField, NumberField, SelectField, ComboboxField, DateField), so parsing (Appendix B.3), dates, searching and read-only behave exactly as in forms; each is named "Quantity, line 3" (`messages['grid.cell']`). `styles.css` takes their frames off inside a cell (`[data-slot='grid-cell']`: no border, background or radius, 28px high, 4px padding, no label gap, the field's own message hidden because the messages stand under the row) and draws the cell's focus and error lines, unlayered so it wins over the utilities. A number cell's typing area is written left to right in every direction (NumberField), so in a right-to-left grid its `text-align` is `start`, which is the cell's end. The keys are `gridKeyAction` (tested: desktop, phone, shortcuts, limits); the grid listens after the field, so a key a field used (Enter choosing in a combobox's open list) is not taken; Radix's closed select would open on Enter, so on a select cell the grid takes Enter first (Space, the arrows or Alt+ArrowDown open it). An unreadable number or date stays in its cell (the owner's decision of P2.3a) and the grid adds its own danger message under the row ("Quantity: Enter a number", `messages['grid.cellMessage']`). New messages: `grid.cell`, `grid.cellMessage`, `grid.addLine`, `grid.removeLine`, `grid.insertLine`, `grid.deleteLine`, `grid.modifierKey` ("Ctrl"; the application gives "⌘" on a Mac — the grid accepts Ctrl and Cmd everywhere), `grid.enterKey`, `grid.deleteKey`. **Fields gain `describedBy`** (ids added to the control's `aria-describedby`), so each cell points to its row's messages. **"Done when":** the "Ten lines by keyboard" story enters ten full lines without a mouse — the item chosen by typing and Enter, Tab across, Tab to "Add line" and Enter — and checks the total, in light and dark, left-to-right and right-to-left (story tests).

## Form layout

- **2026-10-01 — Form layout (P3.5, decided by the owner; old Design System values where noted).**
  1. **FormSection:** the SectionCard card (title and optional description at the start, actions at the end, no icon, no divider line), the fields in a grid: `columns` 1–3 (default 2), 16px (md) apart, one column on phones; a field can be full width (`FormFullWidth`). **Collapsible** (old behaviour, for rarely used fields): closed by default (`defaultOpen` to override); the header is a button with a 14px chevron (pointing to the end when closed, mirrored in right-to-left; down when open) and the title in 13px (sm), semibold, `text.secondary`; `aria-expanded`.
  2. **A tab with errors** (old behaviour): after the tab's label a 13px AlertTriangle (lucide TriangleAlert) in the danger colour, 6px apart, never wrapping, no number — a shape, not only a red dot, so it does not rely on colour. The tab's accessible name becomes "<label> — has errors" (`messages['form.hasErrors']`). On submit the first tab containing an error is selected and the focus moves to the first invalid field.
  3. **Bottom action bar** (the old RecordFormTemplate): the actions at the top **and** a bar at the bottom. `stickyActions`: 'auto' (default) shows the bar whenever the content actually scrolls — on a form that fits the screen a second set of buttons right under the first reads as a bug — and 'always' / 'never' force it. (Replaced on 2026-10-02, P3.6: 'auto' shows the bar only while the top actions are out of view; see "Polish from the owner's review".) The bar: sticky at the bottom, above the content, on `surface.page` (not raised), **no shadow**, a 1px `border.default` line on top, padding sm (12px), across the form's full width; while the form has unsaved changes, "Unsaved changes" (`messages['form.unsaved']`, 12px, `text.tertiary`) at the start; the actions at the end.
  4. **React Hook Form binding:** in P3.5, in the `@veljaos/ui/form` subpath, with React Hook Form an optional peer dependency pinned exactly and listed in THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. No validation library. The field components stay presentational and do not depend on it.
- **2026-10-01 — Form layout (P3.5), how it is built.**
  - `FormSection` takes `title`, `description`, `actions`, `headingLevel`, `columns`, `collapsible`, `defaultOpen`; a collapsible section keeps its fields mounted while closed (hidden), so a field with an error can be found and revealed.
  - `FormTabs` takes `items` (`FormTab`: value, label, content, `hasErrors`, `disabled`), a controlled or uncontrolled value and a `label`; only the active panel is mounted (AGENTS.md D16).
  - **`focusFirstInvalid(form)`** finds the first invalid field (`aria-invalid`, or a field frame marked invalid). When a tab with errors comes before it — the panels of other tabs are not mounted — that tab is selected first (by focusing it: a Radix tab is chosen on focus or a pointer press, not on a synthetic click) and the focus moves once its panel shows; a field in a closed collapsible section is revealed the same way. The application calls it after a failed save, once the errors are drawn — in an effect after the render, not one animation frame later: under load a frame was not always enough, and the story then focused a later field; with React Hook Form, `useFocusFirstInvalid(formRef, formState)` from `@veljaos/ui/form` does it after each failed submit. FormWizard calls it after a failed step. (A first version had a `revealErrors` counter on FormTabs; React's rules forbid the state change it needed inside an effect, and the DOM helper also covers sections and the wizard.)
  - `FormActions` takes `actions` (an ActionGroup or buttons, shown at the top and in the bar), the content, `stickyActions` and `dirty`. "Scrolls" is measured on the nearest scrolling ancestor, or the page, with a ResizeObserver on the form and that container.
  - `useUnsavedChangesGuard(dirty)` returns `confirmLeave(leave)` and a `dialog` to render: while dirty, closing or reloading the page asks the browser's own question (`beforeunload`), and `confirmLeave` asks with a ConfirmDialog ("Leave without saving?", "Leave" / "Stay", the warning tone with its caution button) before running `leave`. The application calls it from its own links and back button: the Design System knows no router (rule D1).
  - `FormWizard` takes `steps` (`WizardStep`: label, description, content, `validate`), `active`, `onFinish` and `finishLabel`: the Stepper on top, one step mounted at a time (the application keeps the values, so going back loses nothing), Back (intent back) and Next (intent next), Finish (intent confirm) on the last step; Next runs the step's `validate` (sync or async) and stays, focusing the first invalid field, when it fails. A press on an earlier step goes back; on the next step it is Next; further steps do nothing (`wizardStepTarget`, tested).
  - **`@veljaos/ui/form`:** `FormTextField`, `FormTextAreaField`, `FormNumberField`, `FormMoneyField`, `FormDateField`, `FormDateRangeField`, `FormSelectField`, `FormComboboxField`, `FormMultiSelectField`, `FormRadioGroupField`, `FormCheckboxField`, `FormSwitchField` (and `useFocusFirstInvalid`): each is the field of the same name with `control`, `name` and `rules` (React Hook Form's own: required, min, validate …) in place of its value and onChange; it shows the rule's error message and reports changes and blur. Number, money and date fields add one rule of their own: text they cannot read fails with the field's own message, so the form does not submit while it is there (the owner's decision of P2.3a). A second tsup entry builds `dist/form.js` and its types; the package exports `./form`. `pnpm consumer-check` does not import the subpath (its scripts are protected files); the unit tests and the "React Hook Form binding" story cover it. (Since 2026-10-02, P3.6a: consumer-check installs React Hook Form 7.89.0 next to the packed tarballs, type-checks `src/FormExample.tsx` — a `FormTextField` bound with `useForm` from `@veljaos/ui/form` — in its build, and renders one bound field on the server in `check.mjs`.)
  - New messages: `form.hasErrors`, `form.unsaved`, `form.leaveTitle`, `form.leaveMessage`, `form.leave`, `form.stay`, `wizard.back`, `wizard.next`, `wizard.finish`.

## Polish from the owner's review (P3.6)

- **2026-10-02 — Less blue (decided by the owner).** Blue stays only for actions (buttons), links, the focus ring, checked checkboxes (and radios and switches), and the highlighted option in option lists and search (Combobox, MultiSelect, Select, CommandPalette); the owner also kept blue for the active tab line, the selected calendar day and month, and completed Stepper steps. Everything else that showed a selection or a state in blue is neutral:
  - **Tokens.** `surface.selected` is now gray2 (light) and `#363636` (dark), no longer blue0 / rgba(0,120,212,0.18); a new `border.selected` is gray7 (light) and gray4 (dark). Both are in Appendix A.2, which the tokens test reads. The dark value is opaque (the owner accepted "white at 8%"; 8% over the raised surface is exactly `#363636`): translucent, it measured 4.12:1 for text.tertiary over the overlay surface (Appendix B.6). Nothing that stays blue used `surface.selected` (checked: the calendar, the month grid, options and checkboxes use the brand tokens).
  - **Measured** (`packages/tokens/src/contrast.ts`, both themes, every surface; the build fails below the minimum): text.primary on surface.selected at least 10.92 (light) / 10.81 (dark); text.secondary 5.43 / 5.60; text.tertiary 4.67 / 4.58; border.selected against surface.selected and against every surface at least 3:1 (lowest 4.86 light, 7.40 dark); the avatar's text.primary on surface.sunken 10.92 / 16.48.
  - **Selected table row:** surface.selected and a 3px `border.selected` bar on the row's start edge (a pseudo-element of the first cell, so nothing moves). A hovered row stays surface.sunken without the bar, so selected differs from hovered by the bar and the checked checkbox, not by colour alone. **Selected phone card:** surface.selected and a `border.selected` border with a 3px start edge (its start padding 2px smaller, so the content does not move).
  - **Sorted column header:** label and icon in text.primary; an unsorted column's ArrowUpDown icon in text.tertiary, so the sorted column reads by its icon's shape and strength.
  - **BulkActionBar:** surface.sunken with a border.strong border; "N selected" in text.primary; "Select all N" is a neutral subtle xs button with ListChecks, like "Clear all" in FilterBar, no underline. Its action buttons keep their families.
  - **PersonAvatar:** surface.sunken with text.primary initials.
- **2026-10-02 — Text direction from the content (decided by the owner).** In right-to-left pages English sentences rendered broken (".invoices are ready to send 3", ".Unavailable: The period August 2026 is closed"); this happens in the product whenever the application falls back to English for a missing translation. Every text block a component renders — labels, descriptions, errors, disabled reasons, alert, banner, toast, empty-state and dialog texts, tooltips, the unavailable reason, button labels, option and menu labels, tab labels and crumbs, card titles and KeyValueList labels, the grid's row messages, filter pills, the bulk count, "Unsaved changes" — carries `unicode-bidi: plaintext` with `text-align: match-parent` (`TEXT_DIRECTION` in `primitives/classes.ts`, the `bidi-content` utility). `plaintext` takes each paragraph's direction from the first strong character of its own text, so the words keep their order, and isolates an inline element; it leaves `direction`, so the component's layout (the order of icon, text and buttons, flex rows, logical margins) stays the page's. On its own it also moves the text to the side its language starts on — an English accordion title in a right-to-left page drifted to the left, beside the chevron — so `match-parent` resolves `text-align: start` against the parent's direction and the text stays on the page's start side. Chromium and WebKit know only `-webkit-match-parent` (Chromium 154 reports `match-parent` as unsupported), Firefox `match-parent`: the `bidi-content` utility in `styles.css` declares the prefixed one and the plain one inside `@supports`, so the minifier cannot fold them. Text whose element sets its own alignment (centred list messages, the grid's end-aligned headers) takes `plaintext` alone (`TEXT_ISOLATE`). `dir="auto"` was not used because it sets `direction`, which also turns the layout of anything inside the element. Values the application renders itself (table cells, KeyValueList values) are its own; KeyValueList values were already isolated (`bdi`). Each family has an "English in RTL" story (Fields, Actions, Feedback, Overlays, Display, Navigation, Table, Forms, Spinner, Accordion) that checks the computed `unicode-bidi` and `direction` of its texts.
- **2026-10-02 — Alert and Banner default to neutral (decided by the owner).** Without `tone`, an Alert or Banner is `neutral` (the neutral tone's bg and fg, the Info icon, `role="status"`); `info` (blue) only when asked for. **The neutral one has a 1px `status.neutral.border` outline** (owner): its grey is the page's own grey (gray1), so without it the box vanished on the page; it stays visible in a white card too. The other tones keep Mantine's transparent border.
- **2026-10-02 — MultiSelectField clears its search after a choice (owner).** "URgent" stayed in the input after "Urgent" was added. Choosing an option (Enter or a press) now empties the typed text; the list shows every option again and the chosen one stays active. A story checks it.
- **2026-10-02 — DateField check (owner).** "151026" in sr-Latn-RS reads as 2026-10-15; in en-US it is null (month 15), as it should be. No fix was needed; both are unit tests in `parse-and-dates.test.ts`.
- **2026-10-02 — EditableGrid on phones (decided by the owner: the old behaviour).** Below 48em the grid is not a table (it scrolled sideways and was unusable): every line is a card (border.default, radius md, padding sm, raised) with its fields stacked, each under its column's label (12px, text.tertiary, 2px gap; the field keeps its frame, and its own label, "Quantity, line 3", is for screen readers), the line's messages under the fields, and an xs "Remove line N" button (Trash2, destructive family, menu weight) at the card's end. The totals and the footer (the balance) are in a card **sticky at the top** (raised, border.default, radius md, padding xs; each total as label and value with SettlingValue; `totalsLabel` as its title): the balance is the number the operator watches, and on a phone a long entry would push a total at the bottom below the screen. "Add line" stands under the cards, without the shortcut hints. The on-screen keyboard's "next" goes through the line's fields, then into the next line (`gridKeyAction`, unchanged). Without an owner's value: 12px (sm) between cards and between a card's fields, as the card's own padding.
- **2026-10-05 — EditableGrid keeps its state when the layout changes (P3.6).** Playwright's full-page screenshot sets the viewport to 1 × 1px for a moment; the grid (real branching since the phone cards) rendered the cards for that moment and the table again after it, so the visual tests intermittently captured the grid without its focused cell, without unreadable text, or without a total's pending dot (`focusout` logged inside the CI image; the bisection pointed at the phone rewrite). The same happens to a person who resizes a window across 48em or rotates a tablet, so it is fixed in the grid, not in the test: the cell with the focus is remembered (its row and column) and, after a switch, the same cell of the new layout takes the focus with the same selection; the typed text and validity of the number and date cells are kept by the grid (`EntryDraftSlot`, internal); and the time each total became pending is kept (`SettlingSince`, internal), so a SettlingValue rendered anew shows its dot when the first would have and keeps its reserved width (`SettlingMemory`). Story "Layout change keeps typing and focus". **And the layout no longer follows an instant:** `usePhone` answers with the settled viewport, so DataTable, FilterBar and EditableGrid change layout only after the viewport has stayed across 48em for 300ms (`LAYOUT_SETTLE`); a resize or a rotated tablet switches a moment later, and the screenshot's 1 × 1px instant (100–200ms) renders nothing anew. Without it, CI still caught a moved total and, once in 560 repeats, the text cursor. 300ms is not an owner's value: it is longer than the instant measured and too short to notice after a rotation. Proved in the CI image: the visual tests of EditableGrid, SettlingValue and ActionGroup five times each against the new baselines, a script that checks the focus before and after a full-page screenshot, and their story tests three times.
- **2026-10-05 — Direction gaps found in the baseline review (P3.6).** ConfirmDialog titles ("?Delete this item"), DataTable cells, its count and its row-limit note, and the CursorPagination count read wrongly in right-to-left; they now take their direction from their content (cells are isolated, so the cell keeps its alignment). **Not changed:** text typed inside a field keeps the field's direction (".Alfa Trade d.o.o" in a right-to-left TextField): `unicode-bidi: plaintext` on the input also moved the text to the other edge of the field and changed the side of numbers and dates, so it was undone; it is left to the owner.
- **2026-10-05 — Fields after the owner's P3.6 review (P3.6c, decided by the owner).** **Direction inside fields:** the typing area of TextField, TextAreaField, ComboboxField and MultiSelectField, the chosen option of SelectField and MultiSelectField's pills take their direction from their content (`dir="auto"`), so "Alfa Trade d.o.o." and "30 days" read correctly in a right-to-left form; the text stays at the page's start side (`text-align: match-parent`, the utility `text-match-parent`, as text blocks since P3.6), so the field keeps the page layout. This replaces the attempt of the same day with `unicode-bidi: plaintext`, which moved the text to the other edge. Left to right in every language, as already decided for numbers, money and dates (P2.3a): e-mail, telephone and URL fields by their `type`, and — new — any TextField with `direction="ltr"`, for a tax number, an IBAN or a code (the component cannot know which field is a tax number, D5; the application says so); aligned to the field's start as NumberField. **Empty date range:** `DateRangeField` takes `endPlaceholder` (`placeholder` is the start's; nobody used it for both); with both, the empty field reads "From – To" with the dash as faint as the placeholders (`text.tertiary`); with neither, it is empty, without the lone dash. FilterBar passes `filter.from` / `filter.to`. **Number range in FilterBar:** one visible label ("Total") above the pair, so the pair lines up with the other filters (each field keeps its 4px under its hidden label; the row has no margin of its own); "From" and "To" are a `startText` inside each field at its start, drawn as the currency section (Mantine's input section: at least 34px, centred, `text.secondary`), decorative (`aria-hidden`; a press puts the caret in the field); the fields are named `filter.rangeFrom` / `filter.rangeTo` ("Total from" / "Total to"), and a MoneyField adds its currency to the name as it always has ("Total from EUR", P2.3a). The same in the drawer.
- **2026-10-05 — FilterBar amount range (P3.6d, decided by the owner).** "From EUR 100.0" was crowded and cut the number. An amount range now shows its currency once, in the label ("Total (EUR)", `filter.rangeLabel`), and in the fields only "From" / "To" and the number (NumberFields with the provider's money decimals, no currency section); the names are "Total from (EUR)" / "Total to (EUR)" (`filter.rangeFrom` / `filter.rangeTo` take the currency). A long amount is never cut: each field is at least 140px (the width already used for amounts) and grows with its number (the typing area sized by its text, `field-sizing: content`, from an automatic flex basis), and the pair wraps when it no longer fits — in the row and in the 320px drawer. Stories "Long amount" and "Long amount in the drawer" ("1.234.567,89" in sr-Latn-RS) check that every typing area shows all of its text (`scrollWidth <= clientWidth`). A number range without a currency now also starts at 140px (was 100px): with "From" inside, 100px left no room for a number.
- **2026-10-05 — Liro brand assets (P3.7, the owner's logo).** The owner's files (Desktop/liro-logo) are copied into `@veljaos/tokens/brand/` and exported as `@veljaos/tokens/brand/*` (in the package's `files`; the packed tarball holds all 25), the Core's default brand. **Names:** `icon.svg` (brand-blue tile, white mark; identical to `web/favicon.svg`), `icon-light.svg` (light tile, blue mark), `icon-dark.svg` (dark tile, light-blue mark), `icon-dark-white.svg` (dark tile, white mark), `icon-mono-black.svg` / `icon-mono-white.svg` (the mark alone, transparent); `wordmark-light.svg` (brand blue, for light backgrounds), `wordmark-neutral.svg` (the owner's `wordmark-dark`, #323130: a dark wordmark for light backgrounds — renamed because it cannot be read on a dark background), `wordmark-mono-black.svg` / `wordmark-mono-white.svg`, and each wordmark as PNG at 64 and 128 px high; `web/`: `favicon.ico` (16, 32, 48), `favicon.svg`, `apple-touch-icon.png` (the owner's `icon-180.png`, under the name browsers look for), `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `icon-maskable-512.png`, `site.webmanifest` (its icon paths unchanged). The page head the owner gave: `<link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" sizes="48x48">`, `<link rel="icon" href="/favicon.svg" type="image/svg+xml">`, `<link rel="apple-touch-icon" href="/apple-touch-icon.png">`, `<link rel="manifest" href="/site.webmanifest">`, `<meta name="theme-color" content="#0078D4">`. **Checked** (`packages/tokens/src/brand.test.ts`): exactly these files; every SVG has a viewBox and a title, and no live text, fonts, embedded images, data URLs or scripts (the wordmark is outlines, so Space Grotesk is not needed for it; it is shipped anyway as `font-brand`, with its OFL notice since P1.2); PNG and ICO sizes; the manifest's icons exist and one is maskable. The maskable icon's mark reaches 205px from the centre, the safe zone is 204.8px (40% of 512): on its edge. **Reported to the owner:** no coloured wordmark for dark backgrounds (only the white monochrome one); the wordmark is "Liro" alone (no "Business Apps", no icon-and-wordmark lockup). **Use:** no component contains a logo (AGENTS.md D18): AppShell, AuthShell and the status pages take it through props, and the application passes these files. **Storybook:** the brand's `web/` folder is a static directory at the root (Storybook takes its `favicon.svg`), the whole folder is served under `/brand`, and `manager.ts` sets the title "Liro Design System" with `wordmark-light.svg` in the sidebar. "Foundations / Brand" shows every file on light and dark, the icon at 16, 32, 180 and 512 px with the raster files, and the maskable icon with its safe zone.
- **2026-10-06 — Storybook and brand follow-ups (P4.0, the owner's review of P3.7).** **Favicon:** Storybook finds a favicon in a static directory by reading `from:to`; on Windows the colon of the drive letter broke that, so the local catalogue showed Storybook's own pink icon (CI, on Linux, was right). `main.ts` now names `favicon` explicitly (a preset Storybook reads but leaves out of its type, hence the intersection type). **Sidebar wordmark** 28px high (`managerHead` style; Storybook drew the file at its own 72px). **No onboarding checklist** (`features.sidebarOnboardingChecklist` and `menuOnboardingChecklist` off). **Brand page:** each surface shows only the versions made for it, read from the owner's file names — light: wordmark-light, wordmark-neutral, wordmark-mono-black, icon-light, icon-mono-black; dark: wordmark-mono-white, icon-dark, icon-dark-white, icon-mono-white; icon.svg (the brand tile) on both. **No "Liro Business Apps" file** was found in the owner's folder (Desktop/liro-logo), so none was added; the lockup below is the component.
- **2026-10-06 — Brand lockup (P4.0, decided by the owner).** `BrandLockup` is the application's name as text, without an icon: "Liro Business Apps" in the brand face (Space Grotesk), in the logo's colours (`text.logo`, added in P4.1: #0078D4 in light, #3EACEB in dark), one line, the brand name bold and the product name regular. Both names are data (`brandName`, `productName`). Size md (the header) 20px (xl), about the height of the earlier wordmark; size lg (status pages, sign-in) 24px, the h1 size (approved by the owner). **Below 48em only the brand name** (`compact` forces either way). **One link to the home page** (the provider's `linkComponent`), its accessible name the full product name ("Liro Business Apps", `lockupName`), which contains the visible text (WCAG 2.5.3); without `href` a text image with that name. The icon and wordmark files of `@veljaos/tokens/brand/` stay for documents, e-mail, the favicon and the installed app. **History:** the first P4.0 version (the same day) was the icon (28px), the wordmark (20px) and the product name (16px, `text.secondary`), 8px apart; the owner replaced it with text alone.
- **2026-10-06 — The tab favicon (P4.0, decided by the owner).** `web/favicon.svg` (the browser tab only) is made from `icon.svg` without new drawing: no tile, the dots in brand blue, and, because the mark is a field of 177 small dots that blurred into a pale disc at 16px and nearly vanished on a dark tab bar, **a simplified version with fewer, larger dots** — 29 of the original dots, chosen largest first at least 2.2px apart at 16px, each drawn at least 0.8px in radius, keeping the pattern's larger and smaller dots — scaled to fill the favicon (1.25 × the tile's scale, as there is no tile around it). It follows the browser's colour scheme with an embedded rule: blue 6 (#0078D4) in a light browser, blue 4 (#3EACEB, the dark theme's brand colour) in a dark one. `web/favicon.ico` holds 16 and 32px of the simplified version and 48px of the full mark (a Windows shortcut), rendered in Chromium with a transparent background. Checked at 16 and 32px on Chrome's light (#dee1e6) and dark (#202124) tab bars, at 1× and 2×. The installed-app icons (`apple-touch-icon.png`, `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `icon-maskable-512.png`) and `icon.svg` keep the blue tile; `brand.test.ts` checks both.

- **2026-10-06 — Full-screen stories (P4.0).** The Storybook decorator stands every story on the page with 16px around it; a story with `parameters.screen: true` (templates and example screens, Phase 4) fills the frame edge to edge.
- **2026-10-02 — FilterBar (decided by the owner).**
  - **An inline multiSelect keeps the control height (36px).** MultiSelectField has a new `summary` prop: the choices are summed up on one line — the option's label for one, `messages['field.selectedCount']` ("2 selected") for more — in place of the pills, which made the field grow to two lines; the summary is part of the input's description. Choices are changed in the list, or with the pills in the Filters drawer, where the field is not in summary mode.
  - **A number range has visible "From" and "To" labels** (`messages['filter.from' | 'filter.to']`, replacing `filter.rangeFrom` / `filter.rangeTo`, which were only accessible names) under the filter's label. **An optional `currency`** on a numberRange filter makes both ends MoneyFields showing the currency, 140px each inline; without it they are NumberFields, 100px each; decimals per `decimals`. The pill writes the amounts with `format.money`.
  - **Actions above, never below.** The bar's row is `flex-wrap: wrap-reverse`: when the actions do not fit beside search and filters, their line goes ABOVE them, end-aligned. With wrap-reverse the cross axis runs upwards, so the controls align to the bottom with `items-start`. The reading and tab order stays search, filters, actions. In Phase 4 the page's main action goes into the page header and the bar keeps the list's own actions, such as Export (noted in BUILD-PLAN P4.3).
- **2026-10-02 — The form's bottom bar shows only while the top actions are out of view (decided by the owner).** This replaces "whenever the content actually scrolls" (Form layout, P3.5): an IntersectionObserver watches the top actions in their scroll container (or the page), and with `stickyActions` 'auto' the bar is shown only while they are not visible, so the two sets of buttons are never on screen together. 'always' and 'never' are unchanged.
- **2026-10-02 — FormTabs inside the form card (decided by the owner).** FormTabs is now the form's card (the raised surface, border.default, radius lg): the tab list is its first row, start-aligned, its line across the card's width, and the FormSections in a panel are drawn flat inside it (no border, background or radius of their own). Before, the tabs floated centred above a separate card. Page-level Tabs (and the module tabs of Phase 4) keep their own rule: centred (AGENTS.md D16).
- **2026-10-02 — Toggles with several choices (decided by the owner).** The segmented look (blocks merging in a track) is only for one choice (`type="single"`, e.g. Month / Quarter / Year). With `type="multiple"` each option is a separate toggle with **Mantine Chip's values, size sm, with the old system's radius sm (4px), not a pill** (pills are for removable active filters): 28px high, 13px text, padding 20px, a 1px border.default border on the raised surface, surface.hover on hover; pressed: a 12px check in a 20px slot at the start (Chip: icon + spacing / 1.5), padding 10px, surface.selected with a border.selected border — the neutral selection, never blue; 8px (xs) between toggles. The toggle group is a primitive (not exported).
- **2026-10-02 — CommandPalette line (owner).** The line between the search and the results is a thin 1px `border.subtle` (it was border.default, Mantine's gray-2), and the search input clears every browser style (appearance, border, radius, shadow, outline, padding). The "thick black line" could not be reproduced in Chromium (the dev server, the built Storybook and the committed baselines show a 1px grey line); the explicit reset covers a browser that draws the input's own border.
- **2026-10-02 — Progress in a dialog (owner).** A dialog that waits for work ("Sending 24 invoices", not dismissible) shows a Spinner with its text beside it; when the progress is known, a ProgressBar with the count beside it; both in a `role="status"`. Stories "Not dismissible" and "Known progress".
- **2026-10-02 — DataTable refetch loader above the table (decided by the owner).** The 14px loader in the top end corner covered the last header label ("Amount"). It now stands above the table at the end (before the export slot), in a 14px slot that is always reserved, so showing or hiding it never moves the table — the old system also kept it at the top. The slot is a polite live region (`role="status"`): "Updating…" (`messages['table.updating']`) is announced when a refetch starts. The "Refetching" story checks that it stands above the header. The cards use the same slot.
- **2026-10-02 — Spinner and Accordion (new, exported).** **Spinner:** shadcn/ui's spinner (a `role="status"` with a label) drawn as the Design System's own loader, Mantine's "oval": sizes sm 14px, md 20px (default), lg 28px — the loaders of the DataTable refetch, the loading button and the loading toast — in border.brand, still with reduced motion; named by `label` (default `messages['field.loading']`) or by its children, shown 16px beside it (Mantine Group's default gap). **Accordion:** the shadcn/ui accordion on Radix (`primitives/accordion.tsx`, not exported) with Mantine 9.6.2's default Accordion values (`Accordion.css`): a 1px border.default line under each item; the control full width, 16px at the inline sides, a 13px regular text.primary label with 12px above and below, surface.hover on hover; a 15px ChevronDown at the inline end (so on the left in right-to-left), turning 180° in 200ms; the panel 16px padding, 5px at the top; the collapsible's height animation. `Accordion` takes `items` (value, title, content, disabled), `multiple` (default one at a time; the open one closes again), a controlled or uncontrolled `value` (a list in both modes), `onValueChange` and `headingLevel` (default 3). Keyboard per WAI-ARIA through Radix (Enter / Space, ArrowDown / ArrowUp, Home / End), checked by the "Keyboard" story. Closed panels are not mounted, as in Tabs.
- **2026-10-02 — Phase 5 builds on shadcn/ui (owner).** The Message family (P5.1) and the agent interactions (P5.3) are built on the shadcn/ui Radix versions of Bubble, Marker, Message Scroller and Questionnaire (Questionnaire for the questions an agent asks the user); Carousel is not used. Noted in BUILD-PLAN P5.1 and P5.3.
- **2026-10-02 — Messages.** New: `field.selectedCount`, `filter.from`, `filter.to`, `table.updating`. Removed: `filter.rangeFrom`, `filter.rangeTo` (a breaking change of an alpha type, stated in the changeset).

## Templates (Phase 4)

- **2026-10-06 — Application shell (P4.1, decided by the owner; old values where noted).** `AppShell` takes its content as data (`brand` as BrandLockup props, `breadcrumbs`, `commands`, `notifications`, `companies`, `user`, `moduleTabs`) and slots (`impersonationBar`, `environmentMarker`, `offlineIndicator`, `bottomBar`), the page as `children`. **No sidebar navigation, ever:** the launchpad and module tabs navigate (Appendix B.8).
  1. **Header (old values):** 56px, `surface.header`, a 1px `border.default` line under it, horizontal padding md (16px) on phones and lg (24px) from sm; sticky at the top (layer header) together with the impersonation bar above it and the offline indicator under it. **Spacing (owner):** the start group md (16px) — the lockup, the environment marker, from sm a short vertical divider (18px, `border.default`) and the breadcrumbs in xs; the end group: the search button, then md (16px), then the cluster of notifications, company switcher and user menu xs (8px) apart, so search reads as its own control.
  2. **Search (old look):** a neutral "default" button, small (30px, Mantine 'xs', exactly 30px high), the Search icon 15px, `messages['shell.search']` ("Search…"), then 16px (md) and the shortcut as **plain text** in xs `text.tertiary`, no key boxes (owner: the ShortcutHint keys were too big and touched the button's edge) — "Ctrl K" by default (`messages['grid.modifierKey']` and K), `searchShortcut` "⌘K" on a Mac — then the button's normal end padding; width by content; it opens the CommandPalette, which Ctrl/Cmd+K also opens (`aria-keyshortcuts`). On phones an icon-only subtle 36px button, like the bell (owner).
  3. **Notifications (the old behaviour without the pulse):** a subtle 36px button with an 18px Bell; a 10px dot in `status.danger.solid`, its centre 4px in from the bell's top end corner (Mantine Indicator's offset), no number, no animation, hidden when the count is 0. The count is in the button's name (`messages['shell.notifications'](unread)`: "Notifications, 3 unread") and inside the panel, which the application renders (a popover at the end).
  4. **Company switcher:** a subtle neutral 30px button, the company name 13px medium, ChevronDown 14px; below it at the end a 260px list (shadow md): each company with its description (the application's tax number line) in xs `text.tertiary`, an optional waiting count at the end (named `messages['shell.waiting']`), the current one with a 14px check (`aria-current`); a search field at the top when there are more than 7 companies (`COMPANY_SEARCH_THRESHOLD`; name or description, ignoring case and accents, `matchingCompanies`, tested); the order is the application's (recently used first). Highlighted entries take `surface.sunken`, as menu items. **On phones it moves into the user menu:** up to 7 companies as menu items with the check; more as one item "Switch company: …" that opens the same searchable list in a Dialog.
  5. **User menu:** PersonAvatar sm (26px) in a 36px subtle button, no name, named `messages['shell.userMenu']`; the menu below at the end, 220px, radius md, shadow md (old values): the name xs semibold and the e-mail xs `text.tertiary` (left to right, an address), then the application's entries with 14px icons.
  6. **Module tabs:** a second header row 40px high (the header 96px in all), centred (`justify-content: safe center`, so a row wider than a phone starts at its start and scrolls sideways without a scrollbar of its own), the page-level tab look (13px, 16px padding, a 2px line, `border.brand` on the current one); they are links (`linkComponent`), the current one `aria-current="page"`, in a `nav` named `messages['shell.moduleTabs']`.
  7. **Phones** (below 48em by real branching, or `layout`): the lockup without the product name, no breadcrumbs, the icon search, the company switcher inside the user menu, and `bottomBar` for the page's main action within thumb reach: the form's bottom bar (`surface.page`, a 1px `border.default` line on top, padding sm), sticky at the bottom, its content full width, with the safe-area insets (`env(safe-area-inset-*)`) at the bottom and sides; the header adds the top inset.
  8. **A skip link** ("Skip to content", `messages['shell.skipToContent']`) is the first focusable element and leads to the `main` element (WCAG 2.4.1).
  - **Story data (owner's quality bar for Phase 4):** realistic Serbian companies, people, tax numbers, amounts and dates (`templates/shell-story-data.ts`); the interface text stays English. `ExampleProvider` (stories only) keeps the story's locale, direction and theme but writes numbers and dates as a Serbian tenant sees them (12.345,60 RSD, 06.10.2026.) with a fixed today (2026-10-06).
  - **Banner is border-box (fix found in the AppShell review).** Banner is `w-full` with 16px padding and a 1px border; without a reset it was 34px wider than its container (the page scrolled sideways in the "Slots" story). It now says `box-border` itself, as the option lists do (P3.2a).
  - **Breadcrumbs in the header stay on one line:** crumbs that do not fit end with "…" (the separators never shrink).
  - **CommandPalette group headings in normal case (owner, closing the open item):** "Actions", "Go to" in xs (12px), semibold, `text.secondary` — no longer Mantine Spotlight's upper case and bold (P2.6b), which the Phase 4 quality bar forbids for micro-labels.
  - **The lockup in the logo's colours (owner):** a new meaning `text.logo` — blue 6 (#0078D4) in light, blue 4 (#3EACEB) in dark, the colours of the logo files — because the lockup is the logo, not body text; `text.brand` (blue 7 / blue 4) is unchanged. Added to Appendix A.2 (the tokens test reads it); `contrast.ts` measures it on the header and the raised surface (light 4.53:1 on white). **The lockup stands only on those surfaces:** on the grey page (`surface.page`, gray1) #0078D4 reaches 4.05:1 (axe fails it; WCAG exempts logotypes, but the rule stays simple), so the stories show it on the header or the raised surface. The large lockup's 24px is approved.

- **2026-10-06 — Launchpad (P4.2, decided by the owner).** `Launchpad` takes `modules` (id, name, description, icon, `href`, `counter`, `locked`) in the user's order, a `label`, `hidden`, `loading`, `editing` and the callbacks `onReorder`, `onHide`, `onShow`.
  1. **Grid:** one column on phones, two from xs (36em), three from sm (48em), 16px (md) apart (old values).
  2. **Card (old values):** radius lg, padding md, a 1px `border.default` border on the raised surface; the top row holds the 20px icon at the start and the counter at the end; the name sm (13px) bold; an optional one-line description xs `text.secondary`, cut with "…". **The icon square (owner, the old Liro Business App detail):** `surface.sunken`, 10px padding, 10px radius (an owner's value, not a radius token), the icon in `text.primary`. **Counter:** xs, medium, `text.secondary`, plain text, written by the application ("7 to approve"). **Without an owner's value:** 16px (md) between the top row and the name, 2px between the name and the description (as KeyValueList's stacked layout), and every card at least 132px high, the skeleton's height, so loading and loaded cards line up.
  3. **Hover (owner, old detail):** the border turns `border.strong`, no shadow; the icon square turns `brand.solid` with the icon in `brand.onSolid`, which says the card can be opened; colours change in the base duration with the standard easing. Focus: the standard focus ring.
  4. **Locked (owner):** `surface.sunken`, `border.default`, not a link and no hover change; a 12px Lock and the application's text ("Available in Pro") in normal case where the counter stands. It is announced as a link that cannot be followed (`role="link"`, `aria-disabled`). Its icon square is the raised surface, so it stays visible on the sunken card (without an owner's value).
  5. **Keys:** 1–9 open the first nine modules from anywhere on the page — not while typing in a field, inside a dialog or a menu, or with Ctrl, Cmd or Alt (each card carries `aria-keyshortcuts`); the arrows move between cards, left and right in reading order (Appendix B.7), up and down by a row (the columns as laid out), Home and End (`launchpadArrowTarget`, `launchpadDigitTarget`, tested).
  6. **Editing (owner):** the application switches `editing` on and off from its page header ("Customize", then "Done"); each card then shows three 28px buttons — move earlier, move later (arrows that mirror in right-to-left), hide (EyeOff) — named with the module ("Move earlier: Sales") and is not a link; dragging a card onto another's place also reorders, the buttons always remain (WCAG 2.5.7). Hidden modules are listed under the grid as "Hidden (2)" (`messages['launchpad.hidden']`), one line each with a small "Show" button, so nothing is lost. `moveModule` and `dropModule` (tested) compute the new order, which `onReorder` reports. A first version rendered "Done" inside the Launchpad on its own row; the owner's "Customize" belongs to the page header, so "Done" does too.
  7. **Loading:** skeleton cards 132px high (`skeletonCount`, default 6), the grid `aria-busy`.
  8. **`layout`:** 'phone' forces one column (a narrow frame at desktop width, as DataTable's and FilterBar's `layout`); by default the columns follow the viewport. A long counter wraps inside the card, aligned to its end, and never runs past the card's edge (found in the phone-width baseline review).

- **2026-10-06 — List page (P4.3, decided by the owner).**
  1. **ListPage:** the page's main action ("New invoice") on the page background at the end; below it ONE card (raised, `border.default`, radius lg, no shadow) holding the saved views as its first row, the FilterBar (`inCard`) and the table edge to edge (DataTable's new `inCard`: the table reaches the card's edges, the slot above it, the bulk bar, the row-limit note and the count and paging keep 16px from them, 12px under the paging). No other cards. The FilterBar keeps only the list's own actions (Columns, Export).
  2. **No redundant page titles (owner):** where the active module tab already names the page ("Invoices"), the visible title goes; the h1 stays, visually hidden, for screen readers, and the row holds only the main action at the end. **`PageHeader`** (new, exported) carries it: `title`, `titleHidden`, `actions`; ListPage passes `titleHidden` true by default (its prop), WorklistPage false; detail, document, settings and report pages keep a visible title, because there it carries information (a record's number, a person's name) or no tab names the page. The document title ("Invoices – Liro Business Apps") is set by the application's router, not by a component (D1).
  3. **Saved views:** the card's first row, start-aligned (list filters, not page tabs; page-level tabs stay centred, D16), the tab look (40px, 13px, the 2px `border.brand` line under the current one), the count after the label in xs `text.tertiary`, tabular, through `format.number`; the "Save view" slot at the end of the row. They are toggle buttons (`aria-pressed`) in a group named `messages['list.views']`, not tabs: they change the list, they have no panels. The DS only renders them; the Core stores them.
  4. **ColumnChooser** (new, exported): a neutral button "Columns" (Columns3) opening a 280px list (shadow md) of the columns, each with a checkbox (shown or not; a `required` column cannot be hidden) and 28px move up / move down buttons named with the column — reordering without dragging (WCAG 2.5.7); `onChange` reports every column with its order and visibility. Widths without an owner's value: 280px.
  5. **QuickPreview** (new, exported): the Drawer from the end (440px), the record's number as its title, a line under it, a KeyValueList (one column), and Close then "Open" (the next intent, `messages['preview.open']`) at the bottom. **On a list, a click or Space on a row opens it; Enter opens the full page:** DataTable's new `onRowOpen` takes Enter (on rows and phone cards) while the pointer and Space keep `onRowClick`; without `onRowOpen`, Enter presses the row as before, and Space now presses it too (`rowKeyAction`, tested).
- **2026-10-06 — Worklist (P4.3, decided by the owner).** `WorklistPage`: from md (62em) the list 380px at the start and the detail filling the rest, a 1px `border.default` line between them, inside one card, each pane scrolling on its own — the page fills the screen under the shell (`height: calc(100dvh - var(--liro-shell-top))`; AppShell now measures its sticky top — the impersonation bar, header, tabs and offline indicator — with a ResizeObserver and publishes it as `--liro-shell-top`). The detail pane takes the focus only while it scrolls, as DataTable's area (axe `scrollable-region-focusable`).
